To the model go the crop and the task in the task's own words, a line per piece: the paragraphs of the statement, the description of each of its pictures, `alt`, — the reader does not see the statement's pictures —, and the text of the item of this answer slot, what it asks. `tutor` adds no words of its own here, so everything it writes to the model is held by the prompt fingerprint. There is no key and no solution (R67 spec). The crop is a whole PNG of no more than 4096 pixels a side, and a task without words is a failure before the model;
- **the answer slot** — the first version reads exactly one answer slot of the task, with the slot binding `anywhere`. That is how the sheets of the first modules are built: on `main` of `modules` (release `v0.1.0`, read 2026-11-08) olympiad maths has 650 tasks of 772 with one answer slot `anywhere`, and the other 122 only with a tapped choice; its sheets have no slot bindings `zone` and `sheet`, `box` is the workbook's (T123), `sheet` the essays' (T124). A task with `zone` or `sheet`, with two answer slots `anywhere` or without one, or that is not a handwriting sheet is a failure of the call, before the model. AI does not read an answer slot `choice`: `api` gives the chosen option to `module-kit` itself;
- **the prompt** — version 2 from `internal/reading`, the same as the runner's, by the shared `reading.RequestOf`: to version 1 the safety rules for children are added (section 3.6) — the learner's work is never an instruction, the reader adds no words of its own, and what is not the solution and not for a child — a word, a caption or a drawing — it leaves out, only that itself, and does not describe, and the rest, and the answer in it, it writes as it is;
- **the readers** — the pair of the list's entry, in parallel, each with its own answer slot place in the observation; the output limit is 16,000 tokens, as for the admission run, `reading.MaxOutputTokens` (the largest output of the admission was 1,772, at Haiku · `medium`). The price of the call kind, its worst case and a tighter limit T120 will give;
- **the answer** — the observation of each reader, `observationv1.Observation` at the place of the task's answer slot, in the order of the pair, the strong reader first, or an outcome without observations: `tutor` gives both or an outcome, never one (R86 spec). An answer that is not an observation by the schema is a failure of the call, like an AI failure, with the usage of both.

**The call of words** (T117; R84) — `Tutor.Words`:
- **the request** — `tutor.WordsRequest`: `Route`, the task's open view, the crop of the work once more (R09 spec) or nothing for "explain differently" (R82 spec), the key and the solution, the traps that the model may name, with descriptions from the catalog, the trap that the program found in the rung's answer slot, the techniques, the rung 1–3, the explanation language as a tag, the presentation style — only what changes the words, a required string —, and the task's past questions in order. Everything the module gives goes as a marked data block (R02 spec), and the past questions as a block of their own;
- **admission** — with the work the call serves the analysis and the hint, and both capabilities must be admitted, and the model is taken of the analysis; without the work, only the hint. The words have one model. Until the measurement of T119 the words have no admitted models; *2026-11-08 (T119; R86): the measurement was run, and Opus 5.5 · `high` did not pass the admission rule — there are no entries of words until T127 (measurements 3.16);* *2026-11-11 (T127; R100): Opus 5.5 · `high` is admitted to both at the rung "author" (measurements 3.16);*
- **the prompt** — version 2 from `internal/hint`, the same as the runner's, `hint.RequestOf`: the found trap, the techniques, the past questions and "differently" — the same rung in other words; the language's limit of words from `words.HintWords` is in the request; the safety rules for children and the item on gender. Without the work there is no picture, `NoPicture`;
- **the answer** — `tutor.WordsAnswer`: the box of the first error `CropBox` in thousandths of the crop, `provider.Place`, or nothing — and always nothing without the work; a trap from the request, or empty; the tutor's question. An answer not by the schema and a trap not from the request are a failure of the call. The leak, the form (`words.Hint`) and the fallback text are checked and substituted by `api` (section 1).

- **What all the calls share:**
  - the types have no fields for the identifiers of the learner and the account, the pseudonym and the adult's data — other than an own key (R15 spec; privacy 5.1 spec). An own key comes with the request and lives only in the memory of the request (architecture 4.3 spec);
  - each has the AI capability, the AI route and the age category, and with an own key also the key and its provider. By them `tutor` chooses the model and checks admission before the call (section 3.9);
  - the outcome is the model's answer, an AI failure, "limit reached" with a cause (section 3.7) or "not admitted" with a code (section 3.9). An AI failure, "limit reached" and "not admitted" are not the learner's error (R04 spec);
  - with the outcome comes the usage message, an entry per call of a model (section 3.7);
  - `tutor` stores nothing of a call, and its logs are without content (R09 spec).

| Call | Capability | From `api` | To the model | Answer |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| reading a sheet | handwriting reading | the crop of the sheet and the task's open view | the crop and the task's statement, without the key (R67 spec) — to two readers of one provider (R68 spec) | the observation: the parts in order — writing or a drawing, the text as written, whether it is legible, other readings —, and the values of the answer slots as written, with other values (R56 spec). The place is in fractions 0–1000 of the crop, and which place the observation names is decided by epic E3 of `spec` (R53 spec). An answer slot that the readers read differently is ambiguous |
| reading the workbook | handwriting reading | the crops of the filled boxes with their places (the 2026-10-27 amendment to R53 spec) | the crops of the boxes, without the item's text (the 2026-10-28 amendment to R53 spec) — to two readers | per box: the item and the box, the text as written, whether it is legible, other readings, whether it is mirrored. A box with a mirrored digit the observation calls ambiguous, and the verdict is "unreadable" (the 2026-10-29 amendment to R56 spec; version 2 of the prompt, T99) |
| reading an essay | handwriting reading | the crop of the sheet and the task's open view | the crop, without the rubric (R75 spec) and without the topic (section 3.5) — to one reader (the second 2026-11-01 amendment to R68 spec) | the text as written, and whether it can be read: an essay that cannot be read does not reach the assessment (R75 spec) |
| analysis and hint | analysis of the course of solution and hint | the work once more (R09 spec), the task's statement, the task's key and solution, the traps that AI may suppose, the matched trap and the task's techniques (R57 spec), the rung, the presentation style and the explanation language | the crop and the task's statement; the key, the solution, the task's traps with descriptions from the catalog, the matched trap and the techniques — as a marked data block (R02 spec; R57 spec); the rung, the language and the style | the box of the first error in fractions 0–1000, or nothing; a trap from the block, or nothing; the tutor's question. The leak and the form of the question are checked by `api` (R70 spec) |
| assessment by the rubric | assessment by a rubric | the read text, the task's statement as the topic, the rubric's criteria in order, the presentation style and the explanation language | the text as the block `<essay>`, the topic and the criteria in order as a data block (section 3.5), the presentation style and the explanation language; no picture (R75 spec) | per criterion: the number, the justification and whether it is met; one feedback. `api` checks the feedback by the feedback rule (R70 spec) |
| text reading | text reading | — | — | not in the first slice: it has no text canvas (R41 spec). The request type will come with it |
| voice | voice | — | — | stage 3 (section 3.8) |

- **What is still open in the fields:**
  - the call of the E4 measurement had no matched trap and no techniques: a new version of the hint prompt will add them, with its own eval (R38);
  - the observations of reading are not given to the analysis and the hint, as in the E4 measurement (R38): the model sees the crop itself. privacy 5.1 spec lets the observations needed for the words reach the model, and whether they are needed, the eval set of the hint will show;
  - privacy 4 spec lets the past questions of the same task reach the model "for the hint ladder", and the recommendation of О-21 takes them for "explain differently". The hint request will get the field if О-21 is decided by the recommendation;
  - how the reading of an essay gives "unreadable", the prompt of the measurement did not measure: the synthetic sheets are all legible (section 3.5);
  - the analysis and the hint are one call, as on the diagram of architecture 4.1 spec and in the E4 measurement (section 3.4, "Hint"). But concepts 10 spec and the card architecture 3.6 spec give each capability its own prompt and its own eval set. How one prompt holds both is the row В65 «в реестр spec».
- **Functions without a model:**
  - the form check of the hint and the feedback rule are called by `api`, next to the leak check (R70 spec; section 3.5);
  - the admission function and the choice of the model are called by `api` before the attempt, and by `tutor` before each call of the model (section 3.9).

*2026-11-11 (T123; R97): reading the workbook — `Tutor.ReadWorkbook`:*
- *the request — `WorkbookReading`: the open view of the workbook page, the crops of the filled boxes with their places — the item and the box among the item's boxes, from 1, — and the route. An empty box has no crop and does not go to the model; a page without filled boxes does not call the model at all. The model sees the crops and their places, without the item line: the request is "without a label", `workbook.Bare`;*
- *the prompt — version 3 of `internal/workbook`: to version 2 are added the safety rules for children, as at the reading of a sheet (section 3.6). The runner and the library build the request with one function, `workbook.RequestOf`, and the observation with `workbook.ObservationOf`: for each box of the page in the task's order, an empty one is "unreadable, blank";*
- *two readers in parallel, the observation of each or the outcome, as at a sheet: an answer without the slot of a filled box or with two is not an observation, and the reading is a failure;*
- *admission — only an entry of the list with the fingerprint of the workbook prompt: `admission.Table.SelectPrompt`. The pair of the sheet measured another prompt and does not admit the workbook (R70 spec), and synthetic sheets alone give no admission (R50): while there is no entry, the reading of the workbook is "not admitted";*
- *the run of 2026-11-11 — the trial part of T99, 20 pages, Opus 5.5 · `high` and Haiku 5.5 · `medium`, $0.60: version 3 reads no worse than version 2 — "correct" on 188 and 190 boxes of 220 against 185 and 186, false "correct" 0 of 40 for both, false alarms 26 and 16 of 160 against 29 and 18, extra "unreadable" 6 and 9 of 200 against 6 and 11;*
- *the call kinds of the library — `Call`: the sheet, the workbook and the words, each with its own capability and its own fingerprint of the prompt. The entry is chosen by `Tutor.ChooseCall` — `api` calls it before the attempt —, and the cost by `Tutor.Price` by the call kind, and the key check takes the models of each kind. `Choose` by capability remains — the first entry of the capability, whatever its prompt;* *2026-11-13 (T138; R106): the hint without the work, "explain it another way", is the kind `CallHint` — the capability `hint` on the words' prompt, one model, no picture and no expected tokens —, and `api`'s gateway asks `ChooseCall(CallHint)`, not `Choose(hint)`;*
- *the limits that hold the worst cost of the workbook: no more than 20 filled boxes in a request — as many pictures as the Claude adapter sends — and no more than 512 pixels on a side of a box's crop; the captions of the pictures count toward the limit of the text.*

*2026-11-11 (T124; R98): reading an essay — `Tutor.ReadEssay`, the first of the two calls of a task with a rubric (section 3.5):*
- *the request — `EssayReading`: the crop of the sheet, the open view of the task with one slot bound to the whole sheet, and the route. To the model goes the crop, without the topic and the rubric (R75 spec), to one reader — the first model of the entry;*
- *the prompt — of the reading in two calls, version 2 of `internal/essay`: the text as written, the learner's errors not corrected, the title on a separate line; new are `legible`, whether the essay can be read as a whole, and the safety rules for children. The runner in two calls, `essay -two`, builds the reading request with the same function, `essay.ReadRequestOf`, and reads the answer with the same `essay.ReadReading`;*
- *the answer — the observation of the sheet's slot: the text as written; an essay that cannot be read as a whole is "unreadable", and it does not reach the assessment (R75 spec; R86 spec); an empty one is "blank". A word that cannot be read is not marked by a field — Т-37, until the measurement on the essays of volunteers (П-16);*
- *the call kind — `CallEssay`: the capability is handwriting reading, its own fingerprint of the prompt, `essay.ReadFingerprint`. The pair of the sheet does not admit it, and while there is no entry it is "not admitted";*
- *the run of 2026-11-11 — the test part of the essays in two calls, Sonnet 5.5 · `low` without thinking and Haiku 5.5 · `medium`: 0 errors in words of 993 for both, all the learner's errors kept, not one essay read as illegible.*

*2026-11-11 (T125; R99): assessment by the rubric — `Tutor.AssessEssay`, the second of the two calls of a task with a rubric (section 3.5):*
- *the request — `RubricAssessment`: the text read, the task statement as the topic, the rubric's criteria in order, the presentation style, the language of the feedback by its tag, and the route. To the model go the text as a block `<essay>`, the topic and the criteria by number, from 1, — as a data block; there is no picture (R75 spec);*
- *the prompt — of the assessment of the text in two calls, version 3 of `internal/essay`: the feedback is in the language the request names, without the learner's gender (R70 spec), with the safety rules for children. The runner in two calls builds the request with the same function, `essay.TextRequestOf`;*
- *the answer — for each criterion, what in the essay decided it and whether it is met, and one feedback. A criterion that is not in the answer or is named twice is without an assessment; a failure of the call — all without an assessment and without feedback. `api` checks the feedback with the feedback rule, `words.Feedback` (T116; R70 spec);*
- *the item on gender and on safety for the feedback is the judge of words of T118, unchanged: `tutor-eval judge-words -log` of the log of essays in two calls judges each feedback, with the topic of its essay as the task;*
- *the call kind — `CallRubric`, the capability is assessment by the rubric, its own fingerprint, `essay.TextFingerprint`. There is no admission: its target is set by the measurement on the essays of volunteers (R71 spec; П-16), and until it — "not admitted";*
- *the run of 2026-11-11 — the test part of the essays, Sonnet 5.5 · `low` without thinking and Haiku 5.5 · `medium`: 0 false alarms of 66, 0 missed "not met" of 24, κ 1.00; the feedback rule was broken in 2 of 15 for Sonnet — for version 1 in 11 of 45 — and in 0 for Haiku; the judge of words flagged none of the 30 feedbacks.*

### 3.11. One call or two

**2026-11-01** (T06; R59): the former subsection 2.1, moved here word for word. Т-23 is closed by R67 spec — two calls —, and the position of `tutor` is R03 and R46.

- **The point:** to read the work and analyse the course of solution with one call of the model or with two. The question is Т-23 of the register of `spec`: it blocks `tutor`, `api`, privacy 5.1 spec and the measurement of stage 2. The answer is given by the measurement of AI reading, and the measurement is the first code of `tutor` (repositories 6 spec). So what is decided here is how the measurement will answer Т-23 and what `tutor` does until the answer; the answer itself goes «в реестр spec».
- **What is known:**
  - now these are two capabilities and two calls: the reading — without the key, the analysis — with the key and the solution (architecture 3.6, 4.1 spec; privacy 5.1 spec);
  - the options of Т-23: two calls, the reading without the key and the analysis with the key (R38 canvas-api); one call, with the key and a comparison of `value` with `written` (R62 canvas-api);
  - the recommendation of Т-23 is two, until the measurement of stage 2 shows that the key does not shift the reading. A model that sees the key can "read in" the expected answer; the main risk of one call is a false "correct" (R62 canvas-api);
  - in an observation, a value is accepted only if it is visible in the writing itself (concepts 9.1 spec; R62 canvas-api). A false alarm is worse than no check, and at any doubt the verdict is "unreadable", not "not yet" (R04 spec; concepts 9.1 spec);
  - the server does not store the picture, so for the analysis the app sends the work once more (architecture 4.1 spec; R09 spec);
  - the measurement goes by each canvas kind and answer kind, as E2 in canvas-api, on samples that adult volunteers made for eval on the samples page of `canvas`, including imitating children's handwriting (repositories 6 spec; R25 spec; П-16). Samples of children — only with a separate consent (П-10) and before the beta: the target for children's handwriting must be checked on that handwriting itself (product 10 spec). With which key the samples go to the AI provider and when samples of children may go to it is section 1 (R24 spec). The main metric is false alarms; in canvas-api its target is no more than 1 % (product 10 spec). canvas-api never ran its own measurement E2 (sources 5.3): there are no numbers on whether the key shifts the reading;
  - in RUN.md of `spec` the question stands in the epic E3 «Контракты первого среза» — together with the measurement of `tutor`.
- **Options:**
  - two calls (R38 canvas-api). The reading does not see the key, and the verdict is given by it before the analysis. The key and the solution go to the model only for the analysis (privacy 5.1 spec). But there are two calls, and this is costlier and slower;
  - one call, with the key and a comparison of `value` with `written` (R62 canvas-api). It is cheaper and faster, and the comparison catches a value that is not in the writing. But a writing that was "read in" it does not catch, and the key with the solution go to the model at every reading.
- **Decided** (R03): until the measurement — two calls. How the measurement will answer Т-23 and at what result one call is allowed is measurements 1.4. What remains open is the answer itself: the conclusions of E2 will give it. `spec` recorded the plan of the answer in Т-23 with the addition of 2026-10-22, by the row В6 of table 3 of [RUN.md](../RUN.md) (R22). *2026-10-25 (R24): the measurement of the course of solution does not answer Т-23 — a measurement of its own will give the answer later, and until it — two calls.* *2026-10-26 (R28): this measurement is the reading into an observation of the epic E4, with the threshold of R03 (measurements 3.1).* *2026-10-28 (T85; R46): the E4 measurement answered by the threshold of R03 — one call is allowed for all three configurations of Claude, but the threshold did not show the power of the test: on none of the 68 synthetic sheets did the reading with the key differ from the reading without it. The author's decision is two calls, until the threshold is passed on the handwriting of adult volunteers (П-16). The answer goes to Т-23 as the row В37 «в реестр spec».* *2026-10-28 (T89; R49): Т-23 is closed by R67 spec — two calls: the reading without the key, the analysis with the key and the task's solution. One call — only for a model that passes the threshold of R03 on the handwriting of adult volunteers (П-16), by a new decision of `spec` with an edit of privacy 5.1 spec. The card architecture 3.6 spec no longer names this item, and the subsection is closed. The decision is recorded in section 1 for now, and T06 will bring it into section 3 together with the other items of E1.*
- **Task:** T01 — done; the answer to Т-23 is the E4 measurement, T71 and T73 (R28), and the position of `tutor` is R46 (T85). Т-23 is closed — R67 spec (T89).

### 3.12. Cost and time of a check

**2026-11-01** (T06; R59): the former subsection 2.6, moved here word for word — the research of the epic E2 and its dated notes.

- **The point:** how to make a check cheaper and faster — in tokens and in seconds to the answer. This is the author's assignment of 2026-10-23: «как сэкономить токены и увеличить время отклика… исследовать похожие решени на рынке и предложить решения». The market is studied by T41, the options within Claude by T42, and the options beyond Claude and the overall recommendation by T44.
- **What is known — the board** (section 3.3). Before T45 the model answered with an analysis, and from T45 with only an assessment and the place of the error. Since T45 the board gets Claude's answer as a stream and records the time to the first token, the thinking tokens and the SDK's retries:
  - the measurements of the board — the cost and time of an answer with an analysis, the series T40 and T45 of 2026-10-25 and what they showed — are measurements 2.1 (R102; T133);
  - the picture: a crop by the ink with digits of about 32 px costs about 200 tokens at Claude, and the picture of the board about 790 (R08; section 3.1; R51 spec);
  - the prompt cache of Anthropic (sources 1.5, sources 1.6):
    - the common beginning of a request lives in the cache for 5 minutes by default, and for an extra charge an hour; each read extends the term;
    - reading it at Opus 5.5 costs 5 % of the input price;
    - a beginning shorter than 512 tokens is not cached.

    The board has only the beginning in common — the instructions, about 250 tokens by the executor's estimate. The picture goes before the task statement (`provider/claude`), so the common beginning ends at it. The board has nothing to cache.
- **What is known — the market** (T41). Sixteen products that read handwritten writing or a handwritten solution. Everything in the table is what the companies themselves state, except what is marked:
  - "press" — a retelling of the press;
  - "review" — someone else's review without a method;
  - "interview" — the words of an employee in the press;
  - "inference" — the executor's inference from a source.

  "Not published" means: searched and not found. The quotes and dates are sources 2.1.

  | Product | Input | Where it recognizes | What it checks | Time and cost | Two paths |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | MyScript: iink SDK, MyScript Math | ink; in Math also a photo | the SDK for applications — on the device, the cloud one — on the server | recognizes and computes the answer; the AI features of Math run on OpenAI | time not published; the cloud — by agreement | the answer right after "=", the steps — only online |
  | Mathpix | ink and picture | on the servers of Mathpix or on the customer's server | only recognizes | by picture — median under 0.5 s, by ink — 200 ms; $0.002 per request | recognizes in the course of writing or by a one-off request |
  | Google ML Kit Digital Ink | ink with time | on the device | only text and shapes, without mathematics | about 100 ms per line (2020); no server needed | one, on the device |
  | Apple Math Notes | Apple Pencil | on the device (interview) | computes the answer; on a tap on "=" shows what it read | "instantly"; no API for mathematics | one |
  | Microsoft OneNote Math Assistant | ink | in Microsoft's cloud | solves and shows its own steps | not published; no API | the answer at once, the steps — by a button |
  | Samsung Notes | the S Pen | on the device, but one footnote requires the internet | computes the answer after "=" | "instant solutions"; no API found | the answer on the device, the steps — through Circle to Search |
  | 小猿口算, now 小猿AI | photo of a page | on the server, based on text recognition (press, 2019) | answers by the type of task | "1 秒", in the company's words; 3–5 s (review, 2026) | "correct" or "incorrect" at once, then the analysis of errors |
  | 作业帮 | photo | on servers with GPUs, more than 30 neural networks — in the photo search system on which the check also stands (press, 2020) | computational tasks as a whole, accuracy 98 % (press, 2020) | photo recognition — 200 ms (press, 2020) | the check at once, the analysis — later |
  | Photomath | photo | in the cloud, earlier — on the device | recognizes the task, a program solves it | "instantly" | animated analyses with AI — in the paid Plus |
  | Magma Math | ink on a canvas | Gemini — by the list of the Canadian legal entity; on the server (inference) | the answer, and also whether the work is shown | not published | the assessment at once, AI — after an incorrect answer |
  | Gradescope | scan or photo in PDF | on the platform; no generative AI — by a secondary source | groups similar answers, the teacher assesses | not published; no public API | in batches |
  | Khanmigo | photo or scan | models of OpenAI; who reads the picture is not published | the learner's course is checked by an agent: it runs their mathematics in a sandbox with SymPy | only the shifts of its own A/B tests | a pre-check decides whether to call the checking agent |
  | Snorkl | recording of a whiteboard and voice | models of OpenAI (2024) | the answer and the explanation, by a model: a score from 0 to 4 | "instant", no numbers | not published |
  | Goodnotes | handwriting in a notebook | the lines — on the device, AI for mathematics — in the cloud | the lines of the working; in the cloud — solves the task | on the device "real-time"; complex requests to AI — "a few minutes" | the device — without credits, the cloud — for credits |
  | MathGPT.ai | handwritten work, university | not published | solutions and proofs | "in minutes" | in batches |
  | Brisk | photo of handwritten work | not published | remarks and feedback for the teacher | "in seconds" | not published |

  **What follows from this:**
  - **Two paths — at seven of sixteen:** MyScript Math, OneNote, Samsung, 小猿口算, 作业帮, Magma and Goodnotes.
    - The fast path is the answer or "correct" at once: on the device at Goodnotes and, by the Newsroom footnote, at Samsung; on the server within a second at 小猿口算 and 作业帮 — in the words of the company and the press.
    - The slow one is the explanation, the analysis or the hint, online: at MyScript Math the steps are only online, and its AI features run on OpenAI; at Goodnotes, a model on Amazon Bedrock and Wolfram Alpha; at Magma, Gemini after an incorrect answer.
    - Khanmigo chooses the path within one answer: a pre-check decides whether to call the agent that checks mathematics, and this takes off about 0.3 s.
  - **The recognizers themselves are fast:** ML Kit — about 100 ms per line on a phone (2020), Mathpix — 200 ms by ink and a median under 0.5 s by picture.
  - **The children's apps** 小猿口算 and 作业帮, by retellings of the press of 2019–2020, check like this: the picture goes to the server and is recognized. 小猿口算 checks it by the type of task, and 作业帮 checks computational tasks as a whole, on the same photo search system as the search for tasks. They name large models later:
    - 小猿口算 — for diagnosing weak spots;
    - 作业帮 — for checking on its own learning tablet S30 (2024). Where the model runs is not said.
  - **Computing the mathematics is given to a program:** at Khanmigo the checking agent runs the learner's mathematics in a sandbox with SymPy, at Goodnotes Wolfram Alpha computes it, at Photomath a program solves the task. This is close to the order of roles of the platform: a program gives the verdict (R03 spec). But the solution for hints at Khanmigo is written by the model o3-mini, and 作业帮 on the S30 checks with a large model.
  - **Few compare the learner's own course:**
    - Khanmigo — by a program;
    - Goodnotes — the lines, on the device;
    - Snorkl — the explanation, by a model;
    - MathGPT.ai — solutions and proofs, in batches;
    - Magma looks at whether the work is shown.

    The others compute the answer to what is written, assess the answer or solve the task themselves.
  - **Few publish speed as a number:**
    - Mathpix — a median under 0.5 s by picture and 200 ms by ink, "on live traffic";
    - ML Kit — about 100 ms per line on a phone (2020);
    - 小猿口算 — "1 秒" on the site, and someone else's review of 2026 saw 3–5 s;
    - 作业帮 — 200 ms for photo recognition, in a retelling of a talk on the photo search system (2020).

    Khan Academy publishes the shifts of its A/B tests:
    - a short answer of the checking agent — 3 s faster on average;
    - an agent that looks only at what the learner has already done — another 400 ms faster, and the hint gives away the answer half as often;
    - a fast model and a pre-check — 0.3 s each.

    The others give no numbers: some write "instantly" or "real-time", the batch ones "in minutes", and MyScript, Microsoft and Gradescope write nothing.
  - **Only Mathpix publishes the price of a request:**
    - $0.002 per request by ink or by picture, and above a million requests a month $0.0015;
    - a session of live recognition — up to a thousand free, then $0.01 up to a hundred thousand.

    There is no public API for mathematics at Microsoft — the Azure ink recognizer is withdrawn — and at Apple: the PencilKit recognizer is only for text. Gradescope has no public API at all, and at Samsung and Photomath we did not find one.
  - **What was read is shown** by Goodnotes — with a confirmation card, before the computation — and by Apple — on a tap on "=", and the learner clarifies unclear signs themselves.
  - **The prompt cache** is not described in the sources found. The time to the first token separately from the full answer is published only by Khan Academy.
- **What is known — where the seconds go** (T42). The sources are sources 1.6, sources 2.2.
  - **What Anthropic says.**
    - The levers of latency are the model, the length of the request and of the answer, and a streamed answer: "The fewer tokens the model has to process and generate, the faster the response will be".
    - By the row "Comparative latency" in Models overview, Haiku 5.5 is "Fastest", Sonnet 5.5 "Fast", Opus 5.5 "Moderate", Fable 5.1 "Slower". Anthropic publishes no numbers.
    - Thinking is billed as output, even when its text is not returned.
  - **Other people's measurements.**
    - Artificial Analysis measures a workload with a picture: one picture of 1 megapixel, about 1,000 input tokens and 1,000 answer tokens, the median over 72 hours. It counts the output speed by the answer tokens. At Claude it does not separate the time of thinking from the time to the first token of the answer — this is an inference from the data of its pages.
    - OpenRouter shows medians of live traffic: the time to the first token — over the last 30 minutes, with all effort levels and any length of requests. Its speed is counted from the sending of the request, together with the time to the first token and thinking.

    | Model · effort | To the first token, s | Output speed, tokens per second | Source |
    |---|---|---|---|
    | Haiku 5.5 · `low` | 4.05 | 116 | Artificial Analysis, Anthropic's API, picture |
    | Sonnet 5.5 · `low` | 1.17 | 73 | Artificial Analysis, Anthropic's API, picture |
    | Opus 5.5 · `low` | 2.84 | 55 | Artificial Analysis, Anthropic's API, picture |
    | Opus 5.5 · `high` | 7.90 | 57 | Artificial Analysis, Anthropic's API, picture |
    | Fable 5.1 · `low` | 0.87 | 36 | Artificial Analysis, Anthropic's API, picture |
    | Haiku 5.5 | 1.77 | 145 | OpenRouter, slice of 2026-10-25 at about 05:19 UTC |
    | Sonnet 5.5 | 1.72 | 103 | OpenRouter, the same slice |
    | Opus 5.5 | 3.02 | 80 | OpenRouter, the same slice |
    | Opus 5.5, Fast mode | 1.72 | 133 | OpenRouter, the same slice |
    | Fable 5.1 | 3.04 | 65 | OpenRouter, the same slice |

    At Artificial Analysis the effort changes the time to the first token, not the output speed. At Opus 5.5, to the first token is 2.84 s at `low` and 7.90 s at `high`, and the speed at both is 55–57 tokens per second. So the extra time goes to thinking.
  - **On the board this is now measured** (above, "What is known — the board", the series T45), and the estimate of T42 by other people's speeds is not needed. The main thing it guessed right: Haiku 5.5 at `low` thinks — for 101–391 tokens —, and Opus 5.5 at `high` did not think at all on the board's tasks. The time of the pairs without thinking goes mostly before the first token.
  - **What else is in the board's request:**
    - **the fields of the answer went in alphabetical order** before T45. The board's schema was a Go map, and the SDK encodes the request with its own copy of `encoding/json` (`anthropic-sdk-go`, `internal/encoding/json`), which sorts the keys of a map. Anthropic, for its part, keeps the order of the schema, and all the board's fields are required. The model wrote `answer`, `comment`, `first_error_line`, `lines`, `verdict`: the comment and the number of the error before the lines read, and the verdict last. Since T45 the value of `properties` is ready JSON, and the order is the schema's: `verdict`, then `mark` (section 3.3);
    - **structured outputs add their own system prompt**, and it is billed as input. How much it weighs is not published, and the whole input of a T45 answer is 1,762–1,828 tokens. The first request with a new schema waits for the grammar to compile, and then the grammar lies in the cache for 24 hours from the last use;
    - **the SDK repeats a failed call** by default up to two times (`MaxRetries: 2`), and the board's time includes such retries, if there were any. Since T45 the board counts them, and in the series T45 there were none;
    - **before T45 the board recorded** neither the time to the first token nor the thinking tokens. The API's answer carries the thinking tokens (`usage.output_tokens_details.thinking_tokens`), and the time to the first token can be measured only by the call itself, a streamed one. Since T45 the board records both.
- **Options — within Claude** (T42). The cost and the time are for one check on the board; this is the executor's estimate, unless said otherwise. Where "series T45" stands, these are the numbers of the board's log of 2026-10-25 (above, "What is known — the board").

  | Option | What changes | Cost | Time | Calls | What is needed | Risk |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | Haiku 5.5 without thinking — on the board since T45 | thinking at Haiku can be turned off at effort `high` and below; the visible answer remains — at a T45 answer, 16–51 tokens | $0.0002 — series T45 | 1.6 s — series T45 | 1 | a thinking switch on the board | "reasoning-like text" does not get into a T45 answer: the schema has no free text. Accuracy not measured, and in the series T45 on the common picture it marked the wrong line both times |
  | Sonnet 5.5 · `low` | at Artificial Analysis — 1.2 s to the first token and 73 tokens per second | $0.0040 — series T45 | 1.6 s — series T45; no thinking even without the switch | 1 | nothing: the board already offers it | on the board — two answers, with thinking and without, both right |
  | Opus 5.5 in Fast mode | output up to 2.5 times faster. Anthropic says the gain is in the output speed, not in the time to the first token. But thinking is output too: at OpenRouter the time to the first token at Opus in Fast mode is 1.72 s against 3.02 s, on different traffic | twice as costly, about $0.02 | about 3–4 s | 1 | `speed: "fast"` in the adapter | only Opus 5.5; not with Batch; different speeds do not share the cache; ZDR — yes, except Covered Models |
  | Cascade Haiku → Opus | Haiku reads everything, Opus only when Haiku returned "unreadable" | $0.0004 + p × $0.0098, where p is the share of hand-overs | the time of Haiku + p × the time of Opus | 1–2 | a rule of hand-over; the share p comes from the measurement | a confidently wrong reading of Haiku the cascade does not catch, and a false alarm is worse than no check (R04 spec). Handing over by the model's verbal confidence is not allowed: it is advised not to trust it (Т-33) |
  | A shorter answer — on the board since T45 | the reading call has no comment in Russian: 60–90 tokens fewer. The T45 board went further: the answer is only the assessment and the place | at Opus 5.5 · `high` — −$0.0018: $0.0080 instead of $0.0098, series T45 | at Opus 5.5 · `high` — −2.3 s: 2.5 s instead of 4.8 s by the medians, series T45. At Khan Academy a short answer of the agent took off 3 s on average (sources 2.1) | 1 | an edit of the schema | on the board the author loses the comment |
  | Fields in order — on the board since T45 | the lines, the answer, the verdict, the number of the error, the comment — set explicitly. The T45 board has `verdict`, then `mark` | 0 | 0; with a stream the verdict comes before the comment | 1 | the order — by a Go struct with the fields in the needed order or by ready JSON, `json.RawMessage`, as the value of `properties` in the schema's map. The SDK sorts the keys of a map, and leaves the fields of a struct and ready JSON as they are | — |
  | A streamed answer — on the board since T45 | the first tokens come earlier; the full time is, it seems, the same — Anthropic does not write about it. The T45 board gets the answer as a stream for the sake of the measurement: the first token at Opus 5.5 · `high` is 1.6 s of 2.5 s, series T45 | 0 | a visible reaction earlier; the program can judge by the lines as they come | 1 | a stream in the adapter and the channel `api` → `app` | the tutor's words — only whole, after the leak check, and a stream — only for the reading (architecture 3.5 spec; R54 spec); an early verdict changes the contract (architecture 4.1 spec, В24) |
  | Cache of the common beginning | reading the common beginning from the cache costs 5 % of the input price at Opus and 10 % at Haiku, and writing 125 % | at Opus — up to −$0.002 per cache hit, if the common beginning reaches 512 tokens | the gain is noticeable "for long documents", and here it is small | 1 | a common beginning of 512 tokens or more. The board has none: the instructions are about 250 tokens, and together with the task statement in the system prompt about 500 (the executor's estimate). And an explicit cache point after the common beginning | the automatic cache puts the point on the last block of the request, and the learner's picture would go into the cache — new each time, so the cache is only written. A change of effort or speed resets the cache. ZDR — yes |
  | A crop of the picture | about 200 tokens instead of about 790 | at Opus — about −$0.0024 | a little less: Anthropic advises shrinking the picture for the sake of latency | 1 | the canvas gives the crop (architecture 3.2 spec; R51 spec) | small digits are read worse (R08) |
  | Batch — only for the measurement | half the cost: at Opus — $2 and $10 per million tokens | at Opus — about $0.005 | usually under an hour, no more than a day | 1 | an asynchronous run | no ZDR: requests and answers are stored up to 29 days, unless the batch is deleted earlier. By the same argument R06 did not take Fable 5.1 into the measurement, so an amendment to R06 and the consent of the volunteers are needed (П-16) |

  **ZDR of the means:** Fast mode — yes, except Covered Models; the cache — yes; Batch — no. Structured outputs store only the schema, up to 24 hours from the last use. The other options are an ordinary call of the Messages API.

  **Not options:** Priority Tier is no longer sold, and all four models of the board are in its list of exceptions anyway. The region `us` is 1.1 times costlier, and Anthropic does not write about latency in it.
- **Recommendation — within Claude** (T42):
  - **for the board — a small task with code.** The author decides, by rule 4: a series costs cents. What is in it:
    - a streamed call and a log: the time to the first token, the full time, the thinking tokens and the SDK's retries;
    - the fields of the schema — explicitly in order: the lines, the answer, the verdict, the number of the error, the comment;
    - Haiku 5.5 without thinking — one more pair to choose.

    Then a series on the same five tasks: Haiku 5.5 · `low` with thinking and without, Sonnet 5.5 · `low`, Opus 5.5 · `high` and the same in Fast mode. The numbers will replace the estimates above. The series ran on 2026-10-25, without Fast mode: the adapter cannot do it. Its numbers replaced the estimates above;
  - **for the measurement** (R21) — for its planning:
    - measure with a stream the time to the first token and the generation separately, and record the thinking tokens;
    - take the pairs by the numbers of the board, Sonnet 5.5 · `low` — if the board shows it fast and accurate;
    - Batch — no, while the measurement holds to ZDR by R06: Batch stores requests up to 29 days. Otherwise — an amendment to R06 and the consent of the volunteers (П-16);
    - decided at the planning of 2026-10-25 (R24): the stream and the thinking tokens are recorded by the runner (T23), the pairs are Opus 5.5 · `high` and Haiku 5.5 · `low` with thinking and without, Sonnet not taken, Batch — no;
  - **for the product:**
    - in the reading call — without the tutor's words, the lines first, the answer short;
    - a stream — only to the program: the tutor's words go to the device whole, after the leak check (R54 spec). An early verdict by the lines — after the answer to В24;
    - the cache — only with an explicit point after the common beginning, not on the picture;
    - Fast mode — only where a strong model is needed and speed matters, at double the price; it works under ZDR, except Covered Models.

  The overall recommendation is below, after the options beyond Claude (T44).
- **Options — beyond Claude** (T44). The cost and the time are for one check on the board: a picture of 1000 × 600 and about 500 tokens of text, and the answer is 150–250 tokens by the current schema or 30–50 by T45. This is the executor's estimate, unless said otherwise. The sources are sources 1.9, sources 1.10, sources 2.3.
  - **The platform already has two paths.** The verdict waits only for the reading call, without the key, and the strong model — the analysis and the hint, with the key — comes only after "not yet" (architecture 4.1 spec). So the question of this part is who makes the reading call faster and cheaper. «Ученик долго не продвигается» from the author's example is a timer, and that goes against О-8 and О-21: in the platform this corresponds to the hint ladder, which the program raises by incorrect answers (R03 spec).
  - **Screening of fast inference providers.** The questions go in order and up to the first "stop" (RUN.md, T44):
    1. a model with pictures in the provider's own API;
    2. training on the customer's data;
    3. minors;
    4. retention, DPA and region — these are only recorded.

    OpenAI is not in the screening: privacy 5.3 spec has already checked its terms. The providers' terms are our reading, not a legal one.

    | Provider | Model with pictures | Training on data | Minors | Retention, DPA, region | Result |
    |---|---|---|---|---|---|
    | Groq | `qwen/qwen3.8-27b`, only Preview — "for evaluation purposes only" | no, without the customer's permission | applications for minors are provided for, and the customer answers for the law. Whether this is a permission, a lawyer will decide | does not retain by default, ZDR — for any customer. That the DPA covers Preview models, we did not find in the documentation. Stores in the USA | passed, but the model with pictures is only for trial |
    | Together AI | Qwen3.5 9B and three more | only with consent. But by default it stores requests and "may use them for product improvements", and the terms themselves exclude training only under ZDR | silent on end users — "18+ only". In the privacy policy — "not directed to, or intended for anyone under the age of thirteen (13)", and the terms forbid passing "any sensitive personal data" | ZDR can be turned on. No public DPA found, the default region is not named | passed, "18+ only" |
    | Cerebras | `qwen-3.8-27b`, Public Preview — in Cerebras's words, "production-ready" | no | silent — "18+ only". The help says "our products are not intended for minors", but this is not a term of the agreement | by the help and the documentation, it stores neither inputs, nor outputs, nor pictures. But the DPA decides this, and it is in a PDF whose text could not be extracted | passed, "18+ only" |
    | SambaNova | `gemma-4-31B-it`, only Preview — "should not be used in production environments" | no | silent — "18+ only" | stores up to 30 days for abuse checks, no ZDR found. The public DPA has no reservation about Preview | passed, "18+ only", but the model with pictures is only for trial |
    | SiliconFlow | yes | no | one may not pass "any personal information of children under the age of 14" | — | stop at question 3 |
    | Fireworks | Kimi K3 and eight more | no | the input must not contain "any personal information of children under 13" | — | stop at question 3 |
    | Mathpix, a recognizer on the server | handwritten mathematics by picture, but handwritten Cyrillic is not in the list of what is supported | does not write about training on data. By default the pictures are seen by the quality assurance team, and this can be turned off for the whole account | silent — "18+ only" | 30 or 90 days — the pages disagree. DPA — on request, there is an address in the EU | passed, "18+ only" |

    **What follows from this** — the executor's inference, not a legal one:
    - **no one suits children now.** Where the terms are silent, there it is "18+ only": for those under 18, privacy 5.1 spec requires terms that allow them outright. The terms of Groq provide for applications for minors and place the law on the customer. Whether this is a permission in the sense of privacy 5.1 spec, a lawyer will decide, and the model with pictures at Groq is for now only for trial, and no DPA for it was found. SiliconFlow and Fireworks forbid passing personal data of children under 14 and under 13;
    - **for adult learners** the first candidate looks to be Cerebras. The essays module is for any age (R41 spec), so such learners are in the slice.
      - Cerebras considers its model with pictures ready for production. By its help and documentation, it does not store the data, but the DPA has not been read. The output is about 1,850 tokens per second, in its words.
      - It does not publish the time to the first token with a picture.
      - Before working with learners' data, its written confirmation is needed that the line of the help about minors does not concern them;
    - **eval at the providers.** Together, Cerebras and Fireworks forbid benchmarking, and SambaNova forbids publishing results without its consent. This concerns the eval runs of `tutor` and the published reports;
    - **open models have their own licenses.** Qwen3.8 and Gemma 4 are under Apache-2.0, and the model Muse Spark Open, in Meta's words, "is not intended for individuals under the age of 18".
  - **A recognizer on the device.** The canvas is a web component, so the recognizer would work in the browser, and the strokes would stay on the device (privacy 1 spec). Everything that ships with the platform must have a license compatible with Apache-2.0 (R12 spec), and for a model this is the code, the weights and the training data.

    | Candidate | Input | Mathematics | In the browser | Size | Licenses: code / weights / data | Result |
    |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
    | Prompt API in Chrome (Gemini Nano) | a picture from the canvas | not published; no Russian | only in Chrome 148 and newer on a computer; needs more than 4 GB of video memory or 16 GB of memory | about 4 GB, by the press | a Google model | not an option: it is Google's generative AI, and there are no tablets and no Russian. How R47 spec relates to it is В27 |
    | Handwriting Recognition API (WICG) | strokes | no | only on some ChromeOS devices, only English | — | part of the OS | not an option |
    | TexTeller 3.0 | a picture of 448 × 448 | handwritten formulas — in the authors' words | yes, ONNX for transformers.js | about 202 MB | Apache-2.0 / Apache-2.0 / the handwritten part, in the authors' words, was collected from open datasets. By the executor's estimate, these are CROHME, MathWriting and HME100K, and the first two are under NC | does not pass the strict reading of R12 spec — because of the data (В28) |
    | Pix2Text-MFR 1.5 | a picture | handwritten formulas — examples on the card | ONNX | about 120 MB | MIT / MIT / not published. The initial weights are from TrOCR, and from which one exactly is not said. The handwritten TrOCR was fine-tuned on IAM, under NC | cannot be decided: both the data and the initial weights need to be disclosed |
    | an own small network for digits — for the slots of the workbook (R41 spec) | a picture of the slot | digits, the comma, the sign | yes | small, by the executor's estimate | Apache-2.0 / Apache-2.0 / the synthetic sheets of `internal/synth` and the samples of adult volunteers (П-16) | passes even the strict reading of R12 spec (В28). Not researched |

    Not one candidate gives a numerical score, and not one writes anything about ":" and the decimal comma. transformers.js does not return the probabilities of tokens. WebGPU is not everywhere — for example, Firefox for Android does not have it.

    The other open models — PP-FormulaNet, pix2tex, SmolVLM, Florence-2 — read printed formulas, and handwriting at them is unfinished or not claimed. SmolVLM, moreover, has among its prohibitions "Evaluating or scoring individuals (e.g., in employment, education, credit)".
  - **Options:**

    | Option | What changes | Cost | Time | Calls | What is needed | Bounds | Risk |
    |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
    | `gpt-6-luna` with effort `none` | an OpenAI model without reasoning. At Artificial Analysis on the workload with a picture — 1.26 s to the first token and 96.7 tokens per second | about $0.0002 — as at Haiku 5.5 | about 1.6–3.8 s | 1 | set `none` explicitly: by default Luna has `medium`. The adapter `provider/gpt` so far knows only `gpt-6-astra` with `high` | the terms of OpenAI are already checked (privacy 5.3 spec). For children under 13 or under the age of digital consent — only with ZDR on OpenAI's approval, and under 18 — also with filters, monitoring and the disclosure that this is AI. The model — only after eval (architecture 4.6 spec) | how Luna counts the tokens of a picture is not published; without reasoning the accuracy is not measured |
    | Fast mode at OpenAI | the former Priority processing | twice as costly | "up to 2.5×" — a general phrase of the guide, and with a named model only for GPT-5.6 Sol. For Luna and Astra there are no numbers of their own | 1 | `service_tier: "fast"` | compatible with ZDR and data residency where the model allows it | Astra has no SLA, and at a sharp rise of traffic part of the requests goes at the ordinary speed and at the ordinary price |
    | A fast provider | a model with pictures at Cerebras, Groq, SambaNova or Together | about $0.0004–0.003 | the generation — fractions of a second, in the providers' words. The time to the first token with a picture is published only for SambaNova — 2.4 s at Artificial Analysis | 1 | an adapter of the provider, a full review of the terms (В26) and eval | for children — no one: the screening above | at Groq and SambaNova — only Preview; the data go to one more processor, one more agreement is needed (privacy 5.1 spec) |
    | Mathpix | a recognizer by picture on the server | $0.002, and above a million pictures a month $0.0015 | a median under 0.5 s, in its words | 1 | an adapter, quality assurance turned off for the whole account, and a DPA | "18+ only" | handwritten Cyrillic is not in the list; retention — 30 or 90 days |
    | Digits on the device | an own small network reads the slots of the workbook | $0 per check | not measured | 0 | the model in the release of `tutor` with a digest, eval and the answer to В27 and В28 | the device reads, and `module-kit` gives the verdict (R03 spec) | a false "correct": a wrong digit read as the key |
    | Hybrid | a fast reader, and the strong model only when that one did not confirm "correct" | the cost of the fast path plus p × the cost of the slow one. With `gpt-6-luna` and Opus 5.5 at p = 0.1, 0.3 and 0.5 — about $0.0012, $0.0031 and $0.0051 | about 2.6, 3.7 and 4.9 s, and on incorrect answers about 7.7 s | 1–2 | a rule of hand-over; p — only from eval | the fast path only confirms "correct", otherwise the strong reader gives the observation, without the key. Whether to count a disagreement of two readings as "unreadable" is the question of Т-33. The path is not chosen by the model's verbal confidence | a false "correct" of the fast reader the hybrid does not catch |

    p is the share of checks that go to the strong model. Its values are the executor's estimate.
  - **Not options:**
    - SiliconFlow and Fireworks — stop at question 3, above;
    - aggregators and clouds — OpenRouter, Bedrock, Azure: one more processor and one more set of terms. Without a key, OpenRouter does not even show the speed;
    - Flex and Batch at OpenAI are half the cost, but slower and sometimes unavailable, with the error 429. This is for the eval runner, not for a live check;
    - Ultrafast exists only for `gpt-6-astra` and GPT-6.1 Sol. It is six times costlier than ordinary, and the speed is not published;
    - input by strokes at Mathpix and the cloud web SDK of MyScript: the strokes would leave the device (privacy 1 spec; architecture 3.1 spec);
    - Prompt API in Chrome and Handwriting Recognition API — in the table of the device above.
- **Recommendation** — the overall one, together with T42 (T44):
  - **for the board (T45)** — nothing beyond T42 without the author's decision. If the author wants to compare Claude with a model that is not Claude, then it is `gpt-6-luna` with effort `none`. The GPT adapter is already on the board, but it does not yet know Luna and `none`. GPT was not connected to the board (R20), and bringing it back is the author's decision;
  - **for the measurement** (R21) — only Claude. But the log should record what the p of the hybrid will later come from: the agreement of Haiku with Opus on "correct" and the share of "unreadable". The agreement is counted by the report of the measurement of the course of writing (T24; R24);
  - **for the product:**
    - the reading call on a sheet — on Claude, as T42 advises: Haiku 5.5 without thinking and a short answer. The series T45 confirmed its cost and speed — $0.0002 and 1.6 s. But on the common picture it marked the wrong line both times, and Haiku 5.5 with thinking marked it exactly, for $0.0003 and 2.7 s. Only eval can choose between them;
    - the second fast reader — `gpt-6-luna` with effort `none`. The terms of OpenAI are already checked, by price Luna is like Haiku, and by the measurement of Artificial Analysis a check will take about 1.6–3.8 s. For children under 13 or under the age of digital consent — only with ZDR on OpenAI's approval, and under 18 — also with the measures of privacy 5.3 spec. The model — only after eval;
    - fast providers — only for adult learners and only after a full review of the terms (В26) and eval. First Cerebras, and Groq — when its model with pictures leaves Preview and the DPA covers it;
    - the slots of the workbook — digits on the device by an own small network, with a fallback to AI: this is the only path that passes even the strict reading of R12 spec (В28). First the answer to В27 is needed: whose code this is and how far to trust a value from the device;
    - in the hybrid the fast path only confirms "correct". "Not yet" and a low score go to the strong reader, without the key, and it gives the observation. Whether to count a disagreement of two readings as "unreadable" is the question of Т-33, and only eval will give p;
    - `app` does not send the work again if it has not changed since the last check: such a check is not worth a call.
  - **The result in one phrase:** fastest and cheapest of all read Haiku 5.5 without thinking (T42) and `gpt-6-luna` with `none` — about $0.0002 per check. The strong model is needed by the analysis and the hint after "not yet" (architecture 4.1 spec). And the fast providers are closed to children now by their own terms, the ready recognizers on the device by the strict reading of the licenses (В28).
- **2026-10-28 (T89; R49).** *`spec` `v0.3.2` answered everything this subsection gave it — in part otherwise than the recommendation above advises:*
  - *an early verdict by the lines — format v0 does not have it: `module-kit` gives the verdict by the observation as a whole (the 2026-10-28 amendment to R54 spec; В24);*
  - *a fast provider — only after a full check of its terms, when the platform needs it: first Cerebras, then Groq (R65 spec; В26);*
  - *digits on the device — code of `tutor`: the weights with a digest in its release, eval and admission, and the verdict is with `module-kit` (R66 spec; В27). The code, the weights and the training data are of category A, and a sample of an adult volunteer goes into training only if their consent names it (the 2026-10-28 amendment to R12 spec; П-16; В28). The first slice has no such reading. The Prompt API in Chrome is closed, as are Google's models (the 2026-10-28 amendment to R47 spec);*
  - *two readers — Т-33 is closed by R68 spec: handwriting is read by two readers of one provider, without the key, and a slot that they read differently is "unreadable". The pair that the E4 measurement measured is Opus 5.5 · `high` and Haiku 5.5 · `low`, and Haiku without thinking in the pair is rejected: coverage 75 %. A second reader of another provider, `gpt-6-luna`, and the hybrid where the fast path only confirms "correct" did not enter the decision: no one measured a pair of different providers. Which models are in the pair in the product is decided by admission by eval (R07 spec).*
- **2026-10-29 (T98; R51).** *The pair of R68 spec is the same, and the 2026-10-29 amendment to R68 spec names Haiku 5.5 · `medium` the first candidate for the check on the handwriting of adult volunteers (П-16): in the extended measurement the pair of Opus with it released more sheets without errors than with Haiku · `low` (measurements 3.14). The models of the pair are still chosen by admission by eval.*
- **2026-11-01 (T100; R58).** *Two readers — only for slots whose check gives a verdict: a sheet under a rubric is read by one reader (the second 2026-11-01 amendment to R68 spec; R53). So the cost of checking an essay does not double.*
- **Task:** T41 — the market; T42 — within Claude; T44 — beyond Claude and the overall recommendation.

## 4. Sources

The records of the sources are in [sources.md](sources.md), in English: until T132 they stood here (R102; T132). The section stays as a pointer, because the decision log, the archives of `docs/plan/` and `spec` cite it and its blocks, and they are not rewritten. Where each block is now:
- «Google Cloud — условия» — sources 1.1;
- «Google — модели и цены» — sources 1.2;
- «Anthropic» — sources 1.3, sources 1.4, sources 1.5, sources 1.6 and sources 1.7;
- «OpenAI» — sources 1.8;
- «Несовершеннолетние: условия OpenAI и Anthropic» — sources 1.11;
- «Свой ключ: лимиты трат и отказы поставщиков» — sources 1.12, and its «Цены и кеш» — sources 1.14, «Отказы» — sources 1.15 and «Проверка ключа» — sources 1.16;
- «Допущенные модели и хранение у Anthropic» — sources 1.13;
- «Право: почерк и динамика пера» — sources 3.1;
